cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.22)
project(BrumaModernNative LANGUAGES C CXX)
if(NOT ANDROID OR NOT ANDROID_ABI STREQUAL "arm64-v8a")
 message(FATAL_ERROR "This locked recipe requires Android ARM64")
endif()
